&=\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{x}^{\ast},x\rangle}{2})f(x+\dot{x}^{\ast})d\dot{x}^{\ast},\end{split}

(5.2)

for
w=x+x^{\ast}\in W
,
l=x_{l}+x_{l}^{\ast}\in L
, with
x,x_{l}\in X
,
x^{\ast},x^{\ast}_{l}\in X^{\ast}
, and
f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})
with
f^{\prime}|_{X}\in S(X)\subseteq L^{2}(X)
,
f\in L^{1}(W)\cap\mathcal{H}(L)
. Through
\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}
and
\theta_{X^{\ast},L}
, we transfer the action of
\operatorname{Sp}(W)
on
\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})
to
\mathcal{H}(L)
. For
g\in\operatorname{Sp}(W)
, and
f=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(f^{\prime})\in\mathcal{H}(L)
, we can define

\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f^{\prime})]=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)\theta_{X^{\ast},L}(f)].

(5.3)

Under such action, for
g_{1},g_{2}\in\operatorname{Sp}(W)
,

\begin{split}\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1})[\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f]&=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g_{1})\theta_{X^{\ast},L}]([\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f])\\
&=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g_{1})\theta_{X^{\ast},L}\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g_{2})\theta_{X^{\ast},L}(f)])\\
&=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g_{1})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g_{2})\theta_{X^{\ast},L}(f)]\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1}g_{2})f.\end{split}

(5.4)

Under the basis
\{e_{1},\cdots,e_{m};e_{1}^{\ast},\cdots,e_{m}^{\ast}\}
of
W
, we identity
\operatorname{Sp}(W)
with
\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, and
\operatorname{Sp}(L)
with
\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
.

5.2.
Explicit Elements I

Case
1
:
g=u(b)\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, with
b=b^{T}
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f^{\prime})(x+l)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle(x+l),(x+l)b\rangle)f^{\prime}(x+l)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{{X^{\ast}}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle(x+l),(x+l)b\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x+l\rangle}{2})f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x+l\rangle}{2})f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}+xb\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}+xb\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=f(wg).\end{split}

Case
2
:
g=h(a)\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f^{\prime})(x+l)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}|\det(a)|^{1/2}f^{\prime}((x+l)a)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},(x+l)a\rangle}{2})f(xa+la+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},(x+l)a\rangle}{2})f(xa+la+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle la,x^{\ast}(a^{\ast})^{-1}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle xa,x^{\ast}(a^{\ast})^{-1}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},xa+l\rangle}{2})f(xa+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}(a^{\ast})^{-1}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle xa,x^{\ast}(a^{\ast})^{-1}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=f(wg).\end{split}

Case 3:
g=\omega_{S}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
. Let
w=x+x^{\ast}
, for
x=x_{s}+x_{s^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{X}_{S}\oplus\mathcal{X}_{S^{\prime}}
,
x^{\ast}=x^{\ast}_{s}+x^{\ast}_{s^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{X}^{\ast}_{S}\oplus\mathcal{X}^{\ast}_{S^{\prime}}
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)f^{\prime}](x+l)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}}\psi(\langle x_{s}+l_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle)f^{\prime}((x_{s^{\prime}}+l_{s^{\prime}})\omega_{S}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}}\psi(\langle x_{s}+l_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle)f^{\prime}((x_{s^{\prime}}+l_{s^{\prime}})\omega_{S}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\langle x_{s}+l_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x_{s^{\prime}}+l_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})f(x_{s^{\prime}}+l_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}dz_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\langle x_{s}+l_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})f(x_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(-\langle l_{s^{\prime}},\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}dz_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle x_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x_{s^{\prime}}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\cdot[\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\psi(-\langle l_{s^{\prime}},\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\langle l_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle)]d\dot{y}^{\ast}dz_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\int_{L\cap X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}/X_{S}^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}\omega_{S}+(-x_{s}^{\ast}+z_{s}^{\ast})\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x_{s^{\prime}}^{\ast})\psi(\langle x_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x_{s^{\prime}}^{\ast},x_{s^{\prime}}+(-x_{s}^{\ast}+z_{s}^{\ast})\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}d\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{\langle x_{s},x_{s}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\int_{L\cap X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}/X_{S}^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}\omega_{S}+x_{s}^{\ast}\omega_{S}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x_{s^{\prime}}^{\ast}\omega_{S})\psi(\langle x_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast},(-x_{s}^{\ast}+z_{s}^{\ast})\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}d\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{\langle x_{s},x_{s}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\int_{L\cap X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}/X_{S}^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}\omega_{S}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x^{\ast}\omega_{S})\psi(\langle x_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast},(-x_{s}^{\ast}+z_{s}^{\ast})\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}d\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{\langle x_{s},x_{s}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\int_{L\cap X_{S}^{\ast}}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}/X_{S}^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}\omega_{S}+z_{s}^{\ast}\omega^{-1}_{S}+\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x^{\ast}\omega_{S})\psi(\langle x_{s},z_{s}^{\ast}\rangle))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast},(-x_{s}^{\ast}+z_{s}^{\ast})\omega^{-1}_{S}\rangle}{2})dz_{s}^{\ast}d\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{\langle x_{s}\omega_{S},x_{s}^{\ast}\omega_{S}\rangle}{2})\int_{L\cap X_{S}}\int_{X_{S}^{\ast}/X_{S}^{\ast}\cap L}f(x_{s^{\prime}}\omega_{S}+x_{s^{\prime}}^{\ast}\omega_{S}+l_{s}+\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}+x_{s}^{\ast}\omega_{S})\psi(\langle l_{s},x_{s}\omega_{S}\rangle))\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast},l_{s}+x_{s}^{\ast}\omega_{S}\rangle}{2})dl_{s}d\dot{y}_{s}^{\ast}\\
&=f(w\omega_{S}).\end{split}

Case
4
:
g=u_{-}(c)\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, with
c=c^{T}
. Then
u(-c)=\omega u_{-}(c)\omega^{-1}
.

\begin{split}\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)&=\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1}u(-c)\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1},u(-c))^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u(-c))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u(-c))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),\omega)^{-1}f(wg).\end{split}

(5.5)

Note:

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),\omega)\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c)\omega,\omega^{-1})=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),1)\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega,\omega^{-1})=1,

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c),\omega)^{-1}=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u(-c)\omega,\omega^{-1})=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(u_{-}(c),\omega).

By Prop.
2.14
, for any element
g\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
(
m\geq 2
), it can be expressed as a product of elements of the form
u(b)
,
u_{-}(c)
. Consequently, there exists an element
\epsilon_{g}\in\mu_{8}
, which is a product of Weil’s indices, such that the following equation holds:

\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)=\epsilon_{g}f(wg).

(5.6)

Note that
\epsilon_{g}
is uniquely determined by
(\ref{chapeq5})
. For
g_{1},g_{2}\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, we have:

\displaystyle\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1})\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f(w)

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{g_{1}}\epsilon_{g_{2}}f(wg_{1}g_{2})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1}g_{2})f(w)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})\epsilon_{g_{1}g_{2}}f(wg_{1}g_{2}).

Hence

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=\epsilon_{g_{1}}\epsilon_{g_{2}}\epsilon_{g_{1}g_{2}}^{-1}.

Example 5.1
.

(1)

If
g=u(b)\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, then
\epsilon_{g}=1
.

(2)

If
g=h(b)\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, then
\epsilon_{g}=1
.

(3)

If
g=u_{-}(c)\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
, then
\epsilon_{g}=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(u_{-}(c),\omega)
.

5.3.
Explicit Elements II

We focus on the other elements

g
of the form
g=u(b)\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})\setminus\Gamma_{m}(2)
.

Case 5:
g=u_{ij}=u(b)\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
, for
i\neq j
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{{X^{\ast}}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle(x+l),(x+l)b\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},x+l\rangle}{2})f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle l,lb\rangle)f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(-\tfrac{\langle x+l,\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}+xb)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}+xb\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(-\tfrac{\langle x+l,\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}+xb)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}+xb\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=f(wg).\end{split}

Case 6:
g=u_{ii}=u(b)\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle l,lb\rangle)f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(-\tfrac{\langle x+l,\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}+xb)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}+xb\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(-\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i})\sum_{l\in L\cap X}\int_{X^{\ast}/X^{\ast}\cap L}f(x+l+\dot{y}^{\ast})\psi(-\tfrac{\langle x+l,\dot{y}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})\psi(\langle l,x^{\ast}+xb+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle x,x^{\ast}+xb+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\psi(-\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i})f(x+x^{\ast}+xb+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})\\
&=\psi(-\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i})f(wg+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast}).\end{split}

Case 7:
g=u^{-}_{ii}=\omega^{-1}u_{ii}\omega\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
,
w=(x,x^{\ast})
,
w\omega^{-1}=(x,x^{\ast})\begin{pmatrix}0&1_{m}\\
-1_{m}&0\end{pmatrix}=(-x^{\ast},x)
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii}\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1},u_{ii})^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii},\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{ii})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii},\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{ii})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii},\omega)^{-1}[\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{ii})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)]f(w\omega^{-1})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i}^{\ast})[\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)]f(w\omega^{-1}u_{ii}+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ii},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i}^{\ast})f(wg+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})\\
&=m_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i}^{\ast})f(wg+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})\\
&=e^{-\tfrac{\pi i}{4}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{i}^{\ast})f(wg+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}).\end{split}

Case 8:
g=u^{-}_{ij}=\omega^{-1}u_{ij}\omega\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
, for
i\neq j
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1}u_{ij}\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1},u_{ij})^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ij},\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{ij})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ij},\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{ij})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{ij},\omega)^{-1}f(wg).\end{split}

Case 9:
g=M_{q}^{(jk)}
. (cf. Def.
2.13
) Let
g_{1}=\iota_{(j,k)}(u_{1111}^{1})
and
g_{2}=\iota_{(j,k)}(u_{1111}^{2})
. Then
g=g_{1}g_{2}
. Note:

•

g_{1}=\iota_{(j,k)}(u_{1111}^{1})=u_{jj}(-1)\cdot u_{kk}(-1)
;

•

g_{2}=\iota_{(j,k)}(u_{1111}^{2})=u_{jj}^{-}(1)\cdot u_{kk}^{-}(1)\cdot u_{jk}^{-}(1)=\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1)\omega
.

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1})f(w)\\
&=\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{jj}(-1)\cdot u_{kk}(-1))f(w)\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k})f(wg_{1}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}).\end{split}

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f(w)\\
&=\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1)\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1},u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1))^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1),\omega)^{-1}\\
&\cdot\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2}\omega^{-1},\omega)^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega^{-1})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega^{-1})^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w\omega^{-1})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}^{\ast})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k}^{\ast})\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{jk}(-1))\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k}^{\ast})\pi_{L,\psi}(\omega)f(w\omega^{-1}u_{jj}(-1)u_{kk}(-1)u_{jk}(-1)+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k}^{\ast})f(wg_{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}).\end{split}

\begin{split}&\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)\\
&=\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1}g_{2})f(w)\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{1})\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f(w)\\
&=\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k})\pi_{L,\psi}(g_{2})f(wg_{1}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast})\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{j}^{\ast}+\tfrac{1}{4}x_{k}^{\ast})f(wg-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}).\end{split}

\begin{split}&\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2}\omega)^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2},\omega)^{-1}\\
&=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g,\omega)^{-1}\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{ Lem. }\ref{calculation4}}}{{=}}m_{X^{\ast}}(g)\\
&=e^{-\tfrac{\pi i}{4}}.\end{split}

5.3.1.

According to Lemma
2.4
(2), every element
g
of
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
can be expressed as

g=\gamma p_{1}\omega_{S_{i}}p_{2}
, for
\gamma\in\Gamma_{m}(2)
,
p_{i}\in P_{X^{\ast}}^{\prime}(\mathbb{Z})
. By the above cases (1)—(5), we have:

\pi_{L,\psi}(g)f(w)=\epsilon_{g}f(wg).

(5.7)

for some
\epsilon_{g}\in\mu_{8}
. Hence:

Lemma 5.2
.

The restriction of the cocycle
[\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}]
to the subgroup
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
is trivial, with an explicit trivialization

\epsilon:\Gamma_{m}(1,2)\to\mu_{8};g\longmapsto\epsilon_{g},

such that
\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=\epsilon(g_{1})\epsilon(g_{2})\epsilon(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}
, for all
g_{i}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
.

6.
Intertwining operators II

This section is intended to present an explicit trivialization map for the group
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
in the above lemma
2.4
. A trivialization map for all
m
is given by the two authors in the classical book
[
LiVe
]
. The explicit formula for
m=1
, which refers to the Gauss sum, is what it calculates. For
m\geq 2
, the formula pertains to the symplectic Guass sum, as defined by Styer in
[
Sty1
]
,
[
Sty2
]
.

6.1.
Lion-Vergne’s result

Retaining the notations from Section
3
, let

Y
and
Z
be two Lagrangian subspaces of
W
. Correspondingly, let
(\pi_{Y,\psi},\mathcal{H}(Y))
,

(\pi_{Z,\psi},\mathcal{H}(Z))
be the Heisenberg representations of
\operatorname{H}(W)
associated to
\psi
. Following
[
Pe
]
, we denote by
A_{YZ}
the isomorphism from
Y/Y\cap Z
to
[Z/(Y\cap Z)]^{\ast}
. Let
d\dot{y}
,
d\dot{z}
represent the quotient measures on
Y/(Y\cap Z)
and
Z/(Z\cap Y)
respectively. We define an intertwining operator
\mathcal{F}_{ZY}
as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rccl}\mathcal{F}_{ZY}:&\mathcal{H}(Y)&\longrightarrow&\mathcal{H}(Z);\\
&f&\longmapsto&\mathcal{F}_{ZY}(f)(h)=\int_{Z/Z\cap Y}f([0,z]h)|A_{ZY}|^{1/2}d\dot{z}.\end{array}

Associated to three Lagrangian subspaces
X,Y,Z
, there exists the Leray invariant
L(X,Y,Z)
.(cf.
[
MVW
, p.55]
) We will denote the quadratic form associated by
q(X,Y,Z)
. Let
\gamma(q(X,Y,Z))
denote the corresponding Weil index.

Theorem 6.1
.

\gamma(q(X,Y,Z))\cdot\mathcal{F}_{XZ}\circ\mathcal{F}_{ZY}\circ\mathcal{F}_{YX}=\operatorname{Id}_{\mathcal{H}(X)}
.

Proof.

See
[
Pe
, Theorem 1.4]
.
∎

Now, consider a Lagrangian subspace
X^{\ast}
of
W
. For
g_{1},g_{2}\in\operatorname{Sp}(W)
, denote
q_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=q(X^{\ast},X^{\ast}g_{2}^{-1},X^{\ast}g_{1})
. Then

\displaystyle\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=\gamma(\psi(q_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2}))).

(6.1)

6.1.1.

Retains the notations from Section
4.1
.
For
g_{1}=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, let
Y^{\ast}=X^{\ast}g_{1}
and
Y=Xg_{1}
. By
[
LiVe
, Section 2.2.19]
(analogously to Section
5.1
), there also exists a pair of explicit isomorphisms between
\mathcal{H}(Y^{\ast})\simeq L^{2}(Y)
and
\mathcal{H}(L)
, given as follows:

\begin{split}\theta_{L,Y^{\ast}}(f^{\prime})(w)&=\sum_{l\in L/L\cap Y^{\ast}}f^{\prime}(w+l)\psi(\tfrac{\langle l,w\rangle}{2}+\tfrac{\langle y_{l},y^{\ast}_{l}\rangle}{2})\\
&=\sum_{l\in L\cap Y}f^{\prime}(y+l)\psi(\langle l,w\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle}{2}),\end{split}

(6.2)

\begin{split}\theta_{Y^{\ast},L}(f)(y)&=\int_{Y^{\ast}/Y^{\ast}\cap L}f([\dot{y}^{\ast},0]\cdot[y,0])d\dot{y}^{\ast}\\
&=\int_{Y^{\ast}/Y^{\ast}\cap L}\psi(\tfrac{\langle\dot{y}^{\ast},y\rangle}{2})f(y+\dot{y}^{\ast})d\dot{y}^{\ast},\end{split}

(6.3)

for
w=y+y^{\ast}\in W
,
l=y_{l}+y_{l}^{\ast}\in L
, with
y,y_{l}\in Y
,
y^{\ast},y^{\ast}_{l}\in Y^{\ast}
, and
f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}(Y^{\ast})
with
f^{\prime}|_{Y}\in S(Y)\subseteq L^{2}(Y)
,
f\in L^{1}(W)\cap\mathcal{H}(L)
.
Let
g_{2}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
and
Z^{\ast}=X^{\ast}g_{2}
. Following
[
LiVe
, p.149]
, let
\beta(Y^{\ast},Z^{\ast})
be an element in
T
such that

\theta_{Y^{\ast},L}\circ\theta_{L,Z^{\ast}}=\beta(Y^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\mathcal{F}_{Y^{\ast}Z^{\ast}}.

Lemma 6.2
.

\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\beta(Z^{\ast},Y^{\ast})\beta(Y^{\ast},X^{\ast})=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2}g_{1}^{-1},g_{1})^{-1}
.

Proof.

\displaystyle 1_{\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})}

\displaystyle=\theta_{X^{\ast},L}\circ\theta_{L,Z^{\ast}}\circ\theta_{Z^{\ast},L}\circ\theta_{L,Y^{\ast}}\circ\theta_{Y^{\ast},L}\circ\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}

\displaystyle=\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\beta(Z^{\ast},Y^{\ast})\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\mathcal{F}_{X^{\ast}Z^{\ast}}\circ\mathcal{F}_{Z^{\ast}Y^{\ast}}\circ\mathcal{F}_{Y^{\ast}X^{\ast}}

\displaystyle=\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\beta(Z^{\ast},Y^{\ast})\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\gamma(\psi(q(X^{\ast},Y^{\ast},Z^{\ast})))^{-1}1_{\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})}.

Hence

\displaystyle\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})\beta(Z^{\ast},Y^{\ast})\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\gamma(\psi(q(X^{\ast},Y^{\ast},Z^{\ast})))=\gamma(\psi(q(X^{\ast},X^{\ast}g_{1},X^{\ast}g_{2})))

\displaystyle=\gamma(\psi(-q(X^{\ast},X^{\ast}g_{1}^{-1},X^{\ast}g_{2}g_{1}^{-1})))

\displaystyle=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2}g_{1}^{-1},g_{1})^{-1}.

∎

For any
g\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, let us define

\widetilde{\beta}(g)=\beta(X^{\ast}g,X^{\ast}).

Lemma 6.3
.

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(h_{1},h_{2})=\widetilde{\beta}(h_{1})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(h_{2})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(h_{1}h_{2})
, for
h_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}a_{i}&b_{i}\\
c_{i}&d_{i}\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
.

Proof.

Write
h_{1}=g_{2}g_{1}^{-1}
,
h_{2}=g_{1}
,
h_{1}h_{2}=g_{2}
. Then:
\widetilde{\beta}(g_{2})^{-1}=\beta(X^{\ast},Z^{\ast})=\widetilde{\beta}([g_{2}g_{1}^{-1}]g_{1})^{-1}
,

\beta(Z^{\ast},Y^{\ast})=\beta(X^{\ast}g_{2}g_{1}^{-1},X^{\ast})=\widetilde{\beta}(g_{2}g_{1}^{-1})
,
\beta(Y^{\ast},X^{\ast})=\widetilde{\beta}(g_{1})
. Hence
\widetilde{\beta}(g_{2})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(g_{2}g_{1}^{-1})\widetilde{\beta}(g_{1})=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2}g_{1}^{-1},g_{1})^{-1}
.
∎

6.2.
The calculation of
\widetilde{\beta}(g)

6.2.1.
\det c\neq 0

Let
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}
with
\det c\neq 0
. Write
Y^{\ast}=X^{\ast}g
. Then
Y^{\ast}
and
X^{\ast}
are two transverse Lagrangian subspaces, and
W=X^{\ast}\oplus Y^{\ast}
. Note that
X^{\ast}\cap L=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast}
and
Y^{\ast}\cap L=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{Z}[e_{i}^{\ast}c+e_{i}^{\ast}d]
. Then

L/[X^{\ast}\cap L+Y^{\ast}\cap L]\simeq[\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{Z}e_{i}]/[\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{Z}(e_{i}^{\ast})c].

The number of elements of
L/[X^{\ast}\cap L+Y^{\ast}\cap L]
is just
|\det c|
. Following
[
LiVe
, Section 2.2.5, Prop.]
,

\widetilde{\beta}(g)=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{l\in L/[(X^{\ast}\cap L)\oplus(X^{\ast}g\cap L)]}e^{-\pi i\langle x_{l},x_{l}^{\ast}\rangle}e^{-\pi i\langle p_{1}(l),p_{2}(l)\rangle},

where
p_{1}:W\to X^{\ast}
and
p_{2}:W\to Y^{\ast}
, and
l=x_{l}+x_{l}^{\ast}\in L=(L\cap X)\oplus(L\cap X^{\ast})
.

For
w=\sum^{m}_{i=1}x_{i}e_{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}x_{j}^{\ast}e_{j}^{\ast}\in W
, let us write
x=(x_{1},\cdots,x_{m})
and
x^{\ast}=(x_{1}^{\ast},\cdots,x_{m}^{\ast})
, and
\underline{e}=(e_{1},\cdots,e_{m})
,
\underline{e}^{\ast}=(e_{1}^{\ast},\cdots,e_{m}^{\ast})
. For
g\in\operatorname{Sp}(W)
, let us write
(\underline{e},\underline{e}^{\ast})^{T}g=A_{g}(\underline{e},\underline{e}^{\ast})^{T}
, for some
A_{g}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
. Identity
g
with
A_{g}
, and write also
A_{g}=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}
.
For
x\underline{e}^{T}\in L
, write

x\underline{e}^{T}=xc^{-1}(c\underline{e}^{T}+d\underline{e}^{\ast T})-xc^{-1}d\underline{e}^{\ast T}.

\displaystyle\widetilde{\beta}(g)=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{x\underline{e}^{T}\in(\mathbb{Z}^{m}\underline{e}^{T})/(\mathbb{Z}^{m}c\underline{e}^{T})}e^{-\pi i\langle-xc^{-1}d\underline{e}^{\ast T},xc^{-1}(c\underline{e}^{T}+d\underline{e}^{\ast T})\rangle}

\displaystyle\quad\quad=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{x\underline{e}^{T}\in(\mathbb{Z}^{m}\underline{e}^{T})/(\mathbb{Z}^{m}c\underline{e}^{T})}e^{-\pi ixc^{-1}dx^{T}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad=|\det c|^{-1/2}\overline{G(d,c)}.

6.2.2.
\det c=0

Set
X^{\ast}\cap Y^{\ast}=M
and
L^{M}=L\cap M^{\bot}+M
. In this case, we consider the non-degenerate symplectic vector space
W^{\prime}=M^{\bot}/M
and
L^{\prime}=L^{M}/M
. According to
[
LiVe
, 2.2.6, Lemma]
,
L^{\prime}
is a self-dual lattice of
W^{\prime}
. Let
X^{\ast^{\prime}}=X^{\ast}/M
,
Y^{\ast^{\prime}}=Y^{\ast}/M
. Then
Y^{\ast^{\prime}}
and
X^{\ast^{\prime}}
are two transverse Lagrangian subspaces of
W^{\prime}
, and
W^{\prime}=X^{\ast^{\prime}}\oplus Y^{\ast^{\prime}}
. Let
|\det c^{\prime}|=|L^{\prime}/[X^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}+Y^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}]|
. Then:

\widetilde{\beta}(g)=|\det c^{\prime}|^{-1/2}\sum_{l\in L^{\prime}/[(X^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime})\oplus(Y^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime})]}e^{-\pi i\langle x_{l},x_{l}^{\ast}\rangle}e^{-\pi i\langle p_{1}(l),p_{2}(l)\rangle},

where
p_{1}:W^{\prime}\to X^{\ast^{\prime}}
and
p_{2}:W^{\prime}\to Y^{\ast^{\prime}}
, and
l=x_{l}+x_{l}^{\ast}\in L^{\prime}\simeq L\cap M^{\bot}/L\cap M
. Moreover, according to
[
LiVe
, 2.2.8, Prop.]
,

\widetilde{\beta}(g)=|\det c^{\prime}|^{-1/2}\sum_{l=x^{\ast}+y^{\ast}\in[L\cap(X^{\ast}+Y^{\ast})]/[(X^{\ast}\cap L)+(Y^{\ast}\cap L)]}e^{-\pi i\langle x_{l},x_{l}^{\ast}\rangle}e^{-\pi i\langle x^{\ast},y^{\ast}\rangle},

where
|\det c^{\prime}|=|L^{\prime}/[X^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}+Y^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}]|=|[L\cap(X^{\ast}+Y^{\ast})]/[(X^{\ast}\cap L)+(Y^{\ast}\cap L)]|
.

Example 6.4
.

If
g\in P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{Z})\cap\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
or
g=\omega_{S_{i}}
, then
\widetilde{\beta}(g)=1
.

Recall
\iota=(\iota_{1},\cdots,\iota_{m}):\operatorname{SL}_{2}(\mathbb{Z})\times\cdots\times\operatorname{SL}_{2}(\mathbb{Z})\longrightarrow\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})
.

Example 6.5
.

If
g\in\iota_{i}(g_{i})
, for
g_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{1}(1,2)\subseteq\operatorname{SL}_{2}(\mathbb{Z})
, with
c\neq 0
, then
\widetilde{\beta}(g)=|\det c|^{-1/2}\overline{G(d,c)}=\widetilde{\beta}(g_{i})
.

Proof.

Y^{\ast}=(\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{m}\mathbb{R}e_{j}^{\ast})\oplus\mathbb{R}(e_{i}^{\ast}c+e_{i}^{\ast}d)
,
X^{\ast}\cap Y^{\ast}=M=(\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{m}\mathbb{R}e_{j}^{\ast})
,
W^{\prime}=\mathbb{R}e_{i}\oplus\mathbb{R}e_{i}^{\ast}
,
L^{\prime}=\mathbb{Z}e_{i}\oplus\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast}
,
X^{\ast^{\prime}}=\mathbb{R}e_{i}^{\ast}
,
Y^{\ast^{\prime}}=\mathbb{R}(e_{i}^{\ast}c+e_{i}^{\ast}d)
,
|\det c^{\prime}|=|L^{\prime}/[X^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}+Y^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}]|=|(\mathbb{Z}e_{i}+\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast})/(\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast}+\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast}c)|=|c|
. For
x=x_{i}e_{i}\in L^{\prime}
, write

x=x_{i}c^{-1}(ce_{i}+de_{i}^{\ast})-x_{i}c^{-1}de_{i}^{\ast}.

\displaystyle\widetilde{\beta}(g)

\displaystyle=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{x_{i}\in\mathbb{Z}/c\mathbb{Z}}e^{-\pi i\langle(-x_{i}c^{-1}d)e_{i}^{\ast},x_{i}c^{-1}(ce_{i}+de_{i}^{\ast}))\rangle}

\displaystyle=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{x_{i}\in\mathbb{Z}/c\mathbb{Z}}e^{-\pi ix_{i}c^{-1}dx_{i}}

\displaystyle=|\det c|^{-1/2}\overline{G(d,c)}.

∎

Example 6.6
.

If
g=u_{ij}^{-}(2t)
, for
i\neq j
,
t\in\mathbb{Z}
, then
\widetilde{\beta}(g)=1
.

Proof.

Y^{\ast}=(\sum_{k=1,k\neq i,j}^{m}\mathbb{R}e_{k}^{\ast})\oplus\mathbb{R}(e_{j}(-2t)+e_{i}^{\ast})\oplus\mathbb{R}(e_{i}(-2t)+e_{j}^{\ast})
,
X^{\ast}\cap Y^{\ast}=M=\sum_{k=1,k\neq i,j}^{m}\mathbb{R}e_{k}^{\ast}
,
W^{\prime}=\mathbb{R}e_{i}\oplus\mathbb{R}e_{i}^{\ast}\oplus\mathbb{R}e_{j}\oplus\mathbb{R}e_{j}^{\ast}
,
L^{\prime}=\mathbb{Z}e_{i}\oplus\mathbb{Z}e_{i}^{\ast}\oplus\mathbb{Z}e_{j}\oplus\mathbb{Z}e_{j}^{\ast}
,
X^{\ast^{\prime}}=\mathbb{R}e_{i}^{\ast}\oplus\mathbb{R}e_{j}^{\ast}
,
Y^{\ast^{\prime}}=\mathbb{R}(e_{j}(-2t)+e_{i}^{\ast})\oplus\mathbb{R}(e_{i}(-2t)+e_{j}^{\ast})
,
|\det c^{\prime}|=|L^{\prime}/[X^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}+Y^{\ast^{\prime}}\cap L^{\prime}]|=|(\mathbb{Z}e_{i}+\mathbb{Z}e_{j})/(2t\mathbb{Z}e_{i}+2t\mathbb{Z}e_{j})|=4|t|^{2}
. For
x=x_{i}e_{i}+x_{j}e_{j}\in L^{\prime}
, write

x=x_{j}(-2t)^{-1}(e_{j}(-2t)+e_{i}^{\ast})-x_{j}(-2t)^{-1}e_{i}^{\ast}+x_{i}(-2t)^{-1}(e_{i}(-2t)+e_{j}^{\ast})-x_{i}(-2t)^{-1}e_{j}^{\ast}.

\displaystyle\widetilde{\beta}(g)

\displaystyle=|\det c|^{-1/2}\sum_{x_{i}\in\mathbb{Z}/2t\mathbb{Z},x_{j}\in\mathbb{Z}/2t\mathbb{Z}}e^{-\pi i\langle x_{j}(2t)^{-1}e_{i}^{\ast}+x_{i}(2t)^{-1}e_{j}^{\ast},x_{j}(-2t)^{-1}(e_{j}(-2t)+e_{i}^{\ast})+x_{i}(-2t)^{-1}(e_{i}(-2t)+e_{j}^{\ast})\rangle}

\displaystyle=|4t^{2}|^{-1/2}\sum_{x_{i}\in\mathbb{Z}/2t\mathbb{Z},x_{j}\in\mathbb{Z}/2t\mathbb{Z}}e^{\pi i\tfrac{1}{t}x_{i}x_{j}}

\displaystyle=1.

∎

Recall the function
\epsilon
from Lemma
5.2
.

Lemma 6.7
.

\widetilde{\beta}(g)\epsilon_{g}=1
, for all
g\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
.

Proof.

For any
g_{1},g_{2}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, we have the following relationship:

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=\widetilde{\beta}(g_{1})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(g_{2})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(g_{1}g_{2})=\epsilon_{g_{1}}\epsilon_{g_{1}}\epsilon_{g_{1}g_{2}}^{-1}.

(6.4)

Thus,
the map
g\to\widetilde{\beta}(g)\epsilon_{g}
defines a character of
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
. Since
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
is generated by
P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{Z})\cap\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
and these
\omega_{S_{i}}
’s, the result follows.
∎

6.3.
Symplectic Gauss Sum

Let us first recall some results of the symplectic Gauss sum from
[
Sty1
]
,
[
Sty2
]
. Let
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, with
\det c\neq 0
. We say two integer vectors
l,l^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}
are congruent modulo
c^{T}
iff
l-l^{\prime}=c^{T}l_{0}
, for some
l_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}
. Following
[
Sty1
]
, we can define the symplectic Gauss sum:

G(d,c)=\sum_{l\bmod c^{T}}e^{\pi i[lc^{-1}dl^{T}]}.

7.
Intertwining operators III

Retain the notations from Examples
3.1
and
3.6
. By
[
Ta1
, Section 3]
or
[
Sa1
, p.401]
, there exists a pair of unitary equivalence between
\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})\simeq L^{2}(X)
and
\mathcal{H}_{F_{\widehat{z}}}
. Here, we provide two explicit unitary equivalence maps derived from their papers. For completeness, we will also present the proofs. Identity
W=X\oplus X^{\ast}
with
\mathbb{R}^{m}\oplus\mathbb{R}^{m}
and denote
q_{\widehat{z}}(v)=e^{-\pi i(v\widehat{z}^{-1}v^{T})}
.

•

\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}:\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})\simeq L^{2}(X)\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{F_{\widehat{z}}}
.

\begin{split}\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}(f)(v)&=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}q_{\widehat{z}}(v+x\widehat{z})^{-1}q_{\widehat{z}}(v)f(x)dx\\
&=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}x\widehat{z}x^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}f(x)dx.\end{split}

(7.1)

•

\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}:\mathcal{H}_{F_{\widehat{z}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}(X^{\ast})\simeq L^{2}(X)
.

\begin{split}\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}(f^{\prime})(x)&=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\overline{q_{\widehat{z}}(v+x\widehat{z})}(\overline{q_{\widehat{z}}(v)})^{-1}k_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}-x\overline{v}^{T}\big]}k_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v).\end{split}

(7.2)

Let us verify that
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}
,
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}
both are
\operatorname{H}(W)
-intertwining operators. Let
h=(y,y^{\ast};t)\in\operatorname{H}(W)
.

1)

\displaystyle\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h)f](v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}x\widehat{z}x^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h)f](x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}x\widehat{z}x^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}\psi(t+\langle x,y^{\ast}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle)f(x+y)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}(x-y)\widehat{z}(x-y)^{T}+(x-y)v^{T}\big)}\psi(t+\langle x-y,y^{\ast}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\psi(t-\tfrac{1}{2}\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\psi\big(\tfrac{1}{2}(x-y)\widehat{z}(x-y)^{T}+(x-y)v^{T}\big)\psi(\langle x,y^{\ast}\rangle)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\psi(t-\tfrac{1}{2}y(y^{\ast})^{T}+\tfrac{1}{2}y\widehat{z}y^{T}-yv^{T})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\psi\big(\tfrac{1}{2}x\widehat{z}x^{T}-x\widehat{z}y^{T}+xv^{T}+x(y^{\ast})^{T}\big)f(x)dx;

\displaystyle\pi_{F_{\widehat{z}},\psi}(h)[\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}f](v)

(7.3)

\displaystyle=\psi(t)e^{-2\pi i[\frac{1}{2}(y\overline{z}+y^{*})y^{T}+vy^{T}]}[\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}f](v+y\overline{z}+y^{\ast})

(7.4)

\displaystyle=\psi(t)e^{-2\pi i[\frac{1}{2}(y\overline{z}+y^{*})y^{T}+vy^{T}]}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}x\widehat{z}x^{T}+x(v+y\overline{z}+y^{\ast})^{T}\big)}f(x)dx

(7.5)

\displaystyle=\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h)f](v).

(7.6)

2)

\displaystyle\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast},v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle v=v_{x}+iv_{y}}}{{=}}e^{-2\pi(v_{y}+yQ_{z})Q_{z}^{-1}(v_{y}+yQ_{z})^{T}}

\displaystyle=e^{-2\pi v_{y}Q_{z}^{-1}v_{y}^{T}}e^{-4\pi v_{y}y^{T}}e^{-2\pi yQ_{z}y^{T}}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)e^{2\pi i(-\overline{v}y^{T}+vy^{T})}e^{2\pi i(\tfrac{1}{2}yzy^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}y\overline{z}y^{T})};

\displaystyle\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}([\pi_{F_{\widehat{z}},\psi}(h)f^{\prime}])(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)[\pi_{F_{\widehat{z}},\psi}(h)f^{\prime}](v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)\psi(t)e^{-2\pi i[\frac{1}{2}(y\overline{z}+y^{*})y^{T}+vy^{T}]}f^{\prime}(v+y\overline{z}+y^{\ast})d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\psi(t)\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}-yz-y^{\ast}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast},v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast})e^{-2\pi i[\frac{1}{2}(y\overline{z}+y^{*})y^{T}+(v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast})y^{T}]}f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\psi(t)\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}-yz-y^{\ast}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast},v-y\overline{z}-y^{\ast})e^{-2\pi i[-\frac{1}{2}(y\overline{z}+y^{*})y^{T}+vy^{T}]}f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\psi(t)\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}-yz-y^{\ast}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)e^{2\pi i[\frac{1}{2}(yz+y^{*})y^{T}-\overline{v}y^{T}]}f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v);

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h)[\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}(f^{\prime})](x)

\displaystyle=\psi(t+\langle x,y^{\ast}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle)[\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}(f^{\prime})](x+y)

\displaystyle=\psi(t+\langle x,y^{\ast}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle y,y^{\ast}\rangle)\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x+y,(x+y)z\rangle-\langle x+y,\overline{v}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\psi(t)\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\psi\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}+x(yz+y^{\ast}-\overline{v})^{T}\big)e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}y(y^{\ast})^{T}+\tfrac{1}{2}yzy^{T}-y\overline{v}^{T}\big)}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)f^{\prime}(v)d_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}([\pi_{F_{\widehat{z}},\psi}(h)f^{\prime}])(x).

Let us verify that
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}
and
\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}
are indeed inverses of one another. It suffices to show the following:

\displaystyle\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}\Big[\theta_{F_{\widehat{z}},X^{\ast}}(f)\Big](x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{2}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xz\rangle-\langle x,\overline{v}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}y\widehat{z}y^{T}+yv^{T}\big)}f(y)dyd_{\widehat{z}}(v)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{2}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}y\widehat{z}y^{T}\big)}f(y)\bigg[\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big[-\langle x,\overline{v}\rangle\big]}\kappa_{\widehat{z}}(v,v)e^{2\pi i\big(yv^{T}\big)}d_{\widehat{z}}(v)\bigg]dy

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle v=v_{x}+iv_{y}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{2}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}y\widehat{z}y^{T}\big)}f(y)\bigg[\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-2\pi v_{y}Q_{z}^{-1}v_{y}^{T}}e^{-2\pi\big((x+y)v_{y}^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi i\big((-x+y)v_{x}^{T}\big)}d_{\widehat{z}}(v)\bigg]dy

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{2}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xP_{z}x^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}yP_{z}y^{T}\big)}e^{\pi(xQ_{z}y^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}xQ_{z}x^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}yQ_{z}y^{T})}f(y)dy

\displaystyle\quad\quad\cdot\bigg[\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-2\pi(\tfrac{1}{2}xQ_{z}+\tfrac{1}{2}yQ_{z}+v_{y})Q_{z}^{-1}(\tfrac{1}{2}xQ_{z}+\tfrac{1}{2}yQ_{z}+v_{y})^{T}}e^{2\pi i\big((-x+y)v_{x}^{T}\big)}d_{\widehat{z}}(v)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xP_{z}x^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}yP_{z}y^{T}\big)}e^{\pi(xQ_{z}y^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}xQ_{z}x^{T}-\tfrac{1}{2}yQ_{z}y^{T})}f(y)dy\bigg[e^{2\pi i\big((-x+y)v_{x}^{T}\big)}dv_{x}\bigg]

\displaystyle=f(x).

In the rest of this section, we assume that
z=\widehat{z_{0}}=z_{0}=i1_{m}
. Retain the notations from Remark
3.7
. Recall the Bargmann transformation from the classical book
[
Fo
, p.40 and p.45]
:

\begin{array}[]{lrcl}\mathcal{B}:&L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{m})&\longrightarrow&\mathcal{H}_{F};\\
&f&\longmapsto&\mathcal{B}(f)(v)=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}f(x)e^{-\pi xx^{T}-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}+2\pi xv^{T}}dx.\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lrcl}\mathcal{B}^{-1}:&\mathcal{H}_{F}&\longrightarrow&L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{m});\\
&\phi&\longmapsto&\mathcal{B}^{-1}(\phi)(x)=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\phi(v)e^{-\pi xx^{T}-\tfrac{\pi}{2}\overline{v}(\overline{v})^{T}+2\pi x\overline{v}^{T}}e^{-\pi\mid v\mid^{2}}dv.\end{array}

Lemma 7.1
.

\mathcal{A}\circ\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}=\mathcal{B}
and
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}\circ\mathcal{A}^{-1}=\mathcal{B}^{-1}
.

Proof.

1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}\circ\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)(v)

\displaystyle=e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)(-vi)

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}ixx^{T}-ixv^{T}\big)}f(x)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}-\pi xx^{T}+2\pi xv^{T}}f(x)dx.

2)

\mathcal{A}^{-1}(\phi)(v)=\phi(vi)e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}};

\displaystyle\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}\circ\mathcal{A}^{-1}(\phi)(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}-x\overline{v}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v,v)\mathcal{A}^{-1}(\phi)(v)d_{z_{0}}(v)

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}ixx^{T}-x\overline{v}^{T}\big]}e^{-2\pi v_{y}v_{y}^{T}}\phi(vi)e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(v)

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}e^{2\pi i\big[-x\overline{v}^{T}-2\pi v_{y}v_{y}^{T}-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}\big]}\phi(vi)d_{z_{0}}(v)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle v^{\prime}=vi}}{{=}}2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}e^{2\pi i\big[-ix\overline{v^{\prime}}^{T}\big]}e^{-2\pi(-v^{\prime}_{x})(-v^{\prime}_{x})^{T}+\tfrac{\pi}{2}v^{\prime}v^{\prime T}}\phi(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}e^{2\pi x\overline{v^{\prime}}^{T}}e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}\overline{v^{\prime}}{\overline{v^{\prime}}}^{T}-\pi\mid v^{\prime}\mid^{2}}\phi(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime}).

∎

Proposition 7.2
.

\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)f\big)=\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)\big(\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)\big)
, for
g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

7.1.
Proof of Proposition
7.2

See
[
Ta1
, Theorem 4.2]
. Let
f=\big(\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{\widehat{z}}}(f^{\prime})\big)
, for
f^{\prime}\in S(\mathbb{R}^{m})\subseteq L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{m})
.

1) Let
g=u(b)
.

\displaystyle\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f)\big)(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\langle x,xb\rangle)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}+\tfrac{1}{2}xbx^{T}\big)}f(x)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z_{0}))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}+\tfrac{1}{2}xbx^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xz_{0}x^{T}-x\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}\big]}k_{\widehat{z_{0}}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{\widehat{z_{0}}}(v^{\prime})dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z_{0}))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}+\tfrac{1}{2}xbx^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xz_{0}x^{T}-x\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}\big]}dx\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z_{0}))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\pi\big(x(21_{m}-bi)x^{T}\big)}e^{2\pi ix\big(v^{T}-\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}\big)}dx\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\to}^{\textrm{ \cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Fo}{}{}, App.A, Thm.1]} }}}2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z_{0}))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[{\det}^{-1/2}(21_{m}-ib)e^{-\pi((v-\overline{v}^{\prime})(21_{m}-ib)^{-1}(v^{T}-\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}))}\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle={\det}^{-1/2}(\tfrac{2i1_{m}+b}{2i})\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[e^{-\pi i((v-\overline{v}^{\prime})(2i1_{m}+b)^{-1}(v^{T}-\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}))}\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime}).

By Example
4.7
(2), we have:

\displaystyle\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)(f^{\prime})(v)

\displaystyle=\gamma(\widehat{z_{0}},\widehat{z_{0}^{\prime}})\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\kappa(\widehat{z_{0}},v;\widehat{z_{0}^{\prime}},v^{\prime})^{-1}f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})\kappa_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})dz_{0}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle={\det}^{-1/2}\big(\tfrac{2i1_{m}+b}{2i}\big)\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi i(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})(2i1_{m}+b)^{-1}(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})^{T}}f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})\kappa_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})dz_{0}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle={\det}^{-1/2}\big(\tfrac{2i1_{m}+b}{2i}\big)2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi i(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})(2i1_{m}+b)^{-1}(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})^{T}}\kappa_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{{}^{\prime}T}\big)}f(x)dxdz_{0}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle={\det}^{-1/2}\big(\tfrac{2i1_{m}+b}{2i}\big)2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}\big)}f(x)dx\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}e^{-\pi i(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})(2i1_{m}+b)^{-1}(v-\overline{v^{\prime}})^{T}}e^{-2\pi v^{\prime}_{y}(v^{\prime}_{y})^{T}}e^{2\pi i(xv^{{}^{\prime}T})}dz_{0}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f)\big)(v).

2) Let
g=h(a)
.

\displaystyle\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f)\big)(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}|\det(a)|^{1/2}f(xa)dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{4}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}|\det(a)|^{1/2}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xaz_{0}a^{T}x^{T}-xa\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})dx

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{2}}|\det(a)|^{1/2}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\pi(x(1_{m}+aa^{T})x^{T}-xx^{T})}e^{2\pi i\big(x(v^{T}-a\overline{v}^{{}^{\prime}T})\big)}dx\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle=2^{\tfrac{m}{2}}|\det(a)|^{1/2}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[{\det}^{-1/2}(1_{m}+aa^{T})e^{-\pi\big((v-\overline{v}^{\prime}a^{T})(1_{m}+aa^{T})^{-1}(v^{T}-(\overline{v}^{\prime}a^{T})^{T})\big)}\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle={\det}^{-1/2}(\tfrac{1_{m}+aa^{T}}{2})|\det(a)|^{1/2}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\Big[e^{-\pi\big((v-\overline{v}^{\prime}a^{T})(1_{m}+aa^{T})^{-1}(v^{T}-(\overline{v}^{\prime}a^{T})^{T})\big)}\Big]k_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime})f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})d_{z_{0}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\to}^{\textrm{Ex. \ref{example232}(1) }}}\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)(f^{\prime})(v)

\displaystyle=\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)\big(\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)\big)(v).

3) Let
g=\omega
.

\displaystyle\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f)\big)(v)

\displaystyle=\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)f(x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+xv^{T}\big)}dx\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi ixy^{T}}f(-y)dy

\displaystyle=\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}f(-y)dy\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xix^{T}+x(v^{T}+y^{T})\big)}dx\Big]

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\to}^{\textrm{ \cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Fo}{}{}, App.A, Thm.1]} }}}\det(2\cdot 1_{m})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}f(-y)dy\Big[e^{-\pi\big((v+y)(v^{T}+y^{T})\big)}\Big];

By Example
4.6
, we have:

\displaystyle\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)\big(\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)\big)(v)

\displaystyle=\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}(f)(v(-1_{m}i))

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\pi iv(-1_{m}i)v^{T}}e^{2\pi i\big(\tfrac{1}{2}xz_{0}x^{T}-xiv^{T}\big)}f(x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\pi vv^{T}}e^{\pi\big(-xx^{T}+2xv^{T}\big)}f(x)dx

\displaystyle=\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}}e^{-\pi vv^{T}}e^{\pi\big(-yy^{T}-2yv^{T}\big)}f(-y)dy

\displaystyle=\theta_{F_{z_{0}},X^{\ast}}\big(\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)(f)\big)(v).

Lemma 7.3
.

(1)

\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})
, for
g_{i}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

(2)

\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})^{-1}=1
, for
g_{i}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

Part (1) is a consequence of the above proposition. Part (2) comes from Lemma
4.2
.
∎

Retain the notations from Example
4.6
.

Lemma 7.4
.

Assume that
m=1
. Let
k_{t}(v)=e^{\pi tv-\tfrac{\pi}{2}t^{2}}
, for
t\in\mathbb{R}
. Then
[\mathcal{B}^{-1}(k_{t})](x)=2^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{-\pi x^{2}+2\pi xt-\pi t^{2}}
.

Proof.

See
[
Zh
, Prop. 6.10]
.
∎

Lemma 7.5
.

Assume that
m=1
. Let
f(v)=v
. Then
[\mathcal{B}^{-1}(f)](x)=2^{\tfrac{5}{4}}xe^{-\pi x^{2}}
.

Proof.

[\mathcal{B}^{-1}(f)](x)=\tfrac{1}{\pi}\tfrac{d}{dt}[\mathcal{B}^{-1}(k_{t})]\mid_{t=0}(x)=\bigg\{\tfrac{1}{\pi}2^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{-\pi x^{2}+2\pi xt-\pi t^{2}}[2\pi x-2\pi t]\bigg\}\mid_{t=0}=2^{\tfrac{5}{4}}xe^{-\pi x^{2}}
.
∎

1)

If we take
f(v)=e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}
, then

\displaystyle\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(f)(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}-x\overline{v}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v,v)e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(v)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{B}^{-1}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{ \cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Fo}{}{}, p.43]} }}}{{=}}2^{m/4}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}.

2)

Take
f_{k}(v)=v_{k}ie^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}
, for
v=(v_{1},\cdots,v_{m})
. We denote
\widehat{v}_{k}=(v_{1},\cdots,v_{k-1},v_{k+1},\cdots,v_{m})
, and
\widehat{x}_{k}=(x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1},x_{k+1},\cdots,x_{m})
.

\displaystyle\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(f_{k})(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m}}\det(2\operatorname{Im}(z))^{\tfrac{1}{4}}v_{k}ie^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}xzx^{T}-x\overline{v}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v,v)e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}vv^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(v)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{C}}2^{\tfrac{1}{4}}v_{k}ie^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}x_{k}x_{k}^{T}-x_{k}\overline{v_{k}}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v_{k},v_{k})e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}v_{k}v_{k}^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(v_{k})\bigg[\int_{\mathbb{C}^{m-1}}2^{\tfrac{m-1}{4}}e^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}\widehat{x}_{k}z\widehat{x}_{k}^{T}-\widehat{x}_{k}\overline{\widehat{v}_{k}}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(\widehat{v}_{k},\widehat{v}_{k})e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}\widehat{v}_{k}\widehat{v}_{k}^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(\widehat{v}_{k})\bigg]

\displaystyle=2^{(m-1)/4}e^{-\pi\widehat{x}_{k}\widehat{x}_{k}^{T}}\int_{\mathbb{C}}2^{\tfrac{1}{4}}v_{k}ie^{2\pi i\big[\tfrac{1}{2}x_{k}x_{k}^{T}-x_{k}\overline{v_{k}}^{T}\big]}k_{z_{0}}(v_{k},v_{k})e^{-\tfrac{\pi}{2}v_{k}v_{k}^{T}}d_{z_{0}}(v_{k})

\displaystyle=2^{(m-1)/4}e^{-\pi\widehat{x}_{k}\widehat{x}_{k}^{T}}\mathcal{B}^{-1}(v_{k})

\displaystyle=2\cdot 2^{m/4}x_{k}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}.

Let us denote
A(x)=e^{-\pi xx^{T}}
,
B_{i}(x)=x_{i}e^{-\pi xx^{T}}
, and
B(x)=\begin{pmatrix}B_{1}(x)\\
\vdots\\
B_{m}(x)\end{pmatrix}
, for
x=(x_{1},\cdots,x_{m})\in X
.

Lemma 7.6
.

Let
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&-b\\
b&a\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})
.

(1)

\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A(x)=A(x)
.

(2)

\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B(x)=(-bi+a)^{T}B(x)
.

Proof.

Let
F(v)=\begin{pmatrix}f_{1}(v)\\
\vdots\\
f_{m}(v)\end{pmatrix}
. By the above 1) and 2),
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(2^{-m/4}f)=A
and
\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(2^{-1}\cdot 2^{-m/4}F)=B
. Hence:

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A

\displaystyle=\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(2^{-m/4}f)

\displaystyle=\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}\Big(2^{-m/4}\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)f\Big)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle Ex.\ref{exm}}}{{=}}\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(2^{-m/4}f)=A;

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B

\displaystyle=\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}(2^{-1}\cdot 2^{-m/4}F)

\displaystyle=\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}\Big(2^{-1}\cdot 2^{-m/4}\pi_{F_{z_{0}},\psi}(g)F\Big)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle Ex.\ref{exm}}}{{=}}\theta_{X^{\ast},F_{z_{0}}}\Big(2^{-1}\cdot 2^{-m/4}(-bi+a)^{T}F\Big)

\displaystyle=(-bi+a)^{T}B.

∎

8.
Siegel modular forms associated to Weil representations

8.1.
Iwasawa decomposition

Let us define:

P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})=\{p=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
0&(a^{T})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}\in P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\mid a\textrm{ is a positive definite symmetric matrix}\}.

Then there exists the positive Iwasawa decomposition:

\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})=P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\cdot\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C}),\quad\quad P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\cap\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})=1_{2m}.

For
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, we write
z_{g}=g(i1_{m})=x_{g}+iy_{g}\in\mathbb{H}_{m}
. Then there exists a positive definite symmetric matrix
\sqrt{y_{g}}
such that
(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{2}=y_{g}
. Let:

•

p_{g}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{y_{g}}&x_{g}(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{-1}\\
0&(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}\in P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})
;

•

k_{g}=p_{g}^{-1}g=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{y_{g}}^{-1}&-(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{-1}x_{g}\\
0&\sqrt{y_{g}}\end{pmatrix}\cdot\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{y_{g}}^{-1}a-(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{-1}x_{g}c&\sqrt{y_{g}}^{-1}b-(\sqrt{y_{g}})^{-1}x_{g}d\\
\sqrt{y_{g}}c&\sqrt{y_{g}}d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})
.

Lemma 8.1
.

(ci+d)(-ci+d)^{T}y_{g}^{T}=1_{m}
.

Proof.

g(i1_{m})=(ai+b)(ci+d)^{-1}=x_{g}+iy_{g}
. Then
(ai+b)=(x_{g}+iy_{g})(ci+d)
.

(ci+d)(-ci+d)^{T}y_{g}^{T}=([cd^{T}-dc^{T}]i+[dd^{T}+cc^{T}])y_{g}^{T}=[dd^{T}+cc^{T}]y_{g}^{T};

(ci+d)\overline{(ai+b)}^{T}=(ci+d)(-ci+d)^{T}(x_{g}-iy_{g})^{T}=[-(ci+d)(-ci+d)^{T}y_{g}^{T}]i+[(ci+d)(-ci+d)^{T}x_{g}];

(ci+d)\overline{(ai+b)}^{T}=(ci+d)(-a^{T}i+b^{T})=(cb^{T}-da^{T})i+(db^{T}+ca^{T})=-1_{m}i+(db^{T}+ca^{T}).

∎

Corollary 8.2
.

\det(ci+d)\det(-ci+d)\det y_{g}=1
and
|\det(ci+d)||\det(-ci+d)|\det y_{g}=1
.

8.2.
Automorphic factor I

Let
z\in\mathbb{H}_{m}
, and
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
. Let us define a cocycle
\beta_{z}^{\prime}
such that
\beta_{z}^{\prime}(g_{1},g_{2})=\beta_{z}(g^{h_{-1}}_{1},g^{h_{-1}}_{2})
, for
g_{1},g_{2}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
. Let
\operatorname{Mp}^{z}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
denote the Metaplectic group associated to the
2
-cocycle
\beta^{\prime}_{z}
of center
T
. Following
[
Ta1
]
, define:

(1)

J(g,z)=cz+d
;

(2)

J_{1/2}(g,z)=\epsilon(g;z,z_{0})\cdot|\det J(g,z)|^{1/2}
;

(3)

J_{3/2}(g,z)=J_{1/2}(g,z)J(g,z)
;

(a)

\alpha_{z}(g)=\frac{\det J(g,z)}{|\det J(g,z)|}
;

(b)

\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})=\{(g,t)\in\operatorname{Mp}^{z}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})\mid t^{2}\alpha_{z}(g)=1\}
.

Lemma 8.3
.

[\beta_{z}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})]^{2}=\alpha_{z}(g_{1})\alpha_{z}(g_{2})\alpha_{z}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}
, for
g_{1},g_{2}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

See
[
Ta1
, p.129]
.
∎

Lemma 8.4
.

If
z=z_{0}
, then:

(1)

\alpha_{z_{0}}(g^{h_{-1}})\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)=1
.

(2)

[\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})]^{-2}=\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{1})\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{2})\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}=[\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})]^{2}
, for
g_{1},g_{2}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

1) For
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, we have
\alpha_{z_{0}}(g^{h_{-1}})=\frac{\det(-ci+d)}{|\det(-ci+d)|}
. By Coro.
8.2
,
\alpha_{z_{0}}(g^{h_{-1}})^{-1}=\frac{\det(ci+d)}{|\det(ci+d)|}=\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)
.

2) By Lemma
7.3
,
\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}=\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})
. Then the result follows directly from the above lemma
8.3
.
∎

Lemma 8.5
.

J_{1/2}(g,z)^{2}=\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)^{-1}\det J(g,z)
and
J_{1/2}(g_{1}g_{2},z)=J_{1/2}(g_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(g_{2},z)\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})
, for
g,g_{i}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

It follows from
[
Ta1
, pp.131-132]
.
∎

Remark 8.6
.

J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})=|\det J(g,z_{0})|^{1/2}=1
, for
g\in\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})
.

Recall the notations
\overline{\operatorname{Sp}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
,
m_{X^{\ast}}
, and
\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}
from Section
4.1
.

Lemma 8.7
.

\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)=[m_{X^{\ast}}(g)]^{-2}
, for
g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

If
z=z_{0}
, then
\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})=\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}=\beta_{z_{0}}(g^{h_{-1}}_{1},g^{h_{-1}}_{2})
. By (
4.2
), we have:

m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-2}m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1})^{2}m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2})^{2}\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{1})\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{2})\alpha_{z_{0}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}=1.

Since
\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
is a perfect group,
\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)=[m_{X^{\ast}}(g)]^{-2}
.
∎

As a consequence,
\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})=\{(g,t)\mid g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R}),t=\pm m_{X^{\ast}}(g)=\pm m_{X^{\ast}}(g^{h_{-1}})^{-1}\}
.

Lemma 8.8
.

There exists a group isomorphism
\iota:\overline{\operatorname{Sp}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})\to\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R});[g,t]\longmapsto[g,m_{X^{\ast}}(g)t]
.

Proof.

\displaystyle\iota([g_{1},t_{1}])\iota([g_{2},t_{2}])

\displaystyle=[g_{1},m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1})t_{1}][g_{2},m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2})t_{2}]

\displaystyle=[g_{1}g_{2},\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1},g_{2})^{-1}m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2})t_{1}t_{2}]

\displaystyle=[g_{1}g_{2},\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2})t_{1}t_{2}]

\displaystyle=[g_{1}g_{2},\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1}g_{2})t_{1}t_{2}]

\displaystyle=\iota([g_{1}g_{2},t_{1}t_{2}\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})])

\displaystyle=\iota([g_{1},t_{1}]\cdot[g_{2},t_{2}]).

∎

Lemma 8.9
.

[J_{1/2}(g,z)m_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-1}]^{2}=\det(cz+d)
, for
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

Proof.

By Lemma
8.5
,
[J_{1/2}(g,z)m_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-1}]^{2}=\det J(g,z)\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)^{-1}m_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-2}=\det J(g,z).

∎

Definition 8.10
.

\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle Def.}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(g,z)m_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-1}
.

Let
g_{3}=g_{1}g_{2}
and
g_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}a_{i}&b_{i}\\
c_{i}&d_{i}\end{pmatrix}
.

Lemma 8.11
.

\sqrt{\det(c_{3}z+d_{3})}=[\sqrt{\det(c_{1}(g_{2}z)+d_{1})}]\cdot[\sqrt{\det(c_{2}z+d_{2})}]\cdot\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})
.

Proof.

Left side

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(g_{1}g_{2},z)m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Lem.}\ref{J121}}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(g_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(g_{2},z)\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(g_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(g_{2},z)\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1}g_{2})^{-1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\ref{chap228inter})}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(g_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(g_{2},z)m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1})^{-1}m_{X^{\ast}}(g_{2})^{-1}\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g_{1},g_{2})

\displaystyle=\text{Right side}.

∎

For later use, let us consider
r\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
and define

\displaystyle\lambda(r)=m_{X^{\ast}}(r)\widetilde{\beta}^{-1}(r).

(8.1)

Lemma 8.12
.

\lambda(r_{1})\lambda(r_{2})=\lambda(r_{1}r_{2})\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1},r_{2})
, for
r_{i}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
.

Proof.

By Lemma
6.3
,
\widetilde{\beta}(r_{1})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(r_{2})^{-1}\widetilde{\beta}(r_{1}r_{2})=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1},r_{2})
. Consequently,

\displaystyle m_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1})\widetilde{\beta}(r_{1})^{-1}m_{X^{\ast}}(r_{2})\widetilde{\beta}(r_{2})^{-1}

\displaystyle=m_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1})m_{X^{\ast}}(r_{2})\widetilde{\beta}(r_{1}r_{2})^{-1}\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1},r_{2})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\beta}(r_{1}r_{2})^{-1}m_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1}r_{2})\overline{c}_{X^{\ast}}(r_{1},r_{2}).

∎

8.3.
Automorphic factor II

For
\widetilde{g}=(g,t)\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, with
g=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, define:

(1)

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)=t^{-1}\cdot\epsilon(g;z,z_{0})\cdot|\det J(g,z)|^{1/2}
;

(2)

J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g},z)=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)J(g,z)
.

Lemma 8.13
.

Let
\widetilde{g},\widetilde{g}_{i}=(g_{i},t_{i})\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
.

(1)

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)^{2}=\det J(g,z)
.

(2)

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1}\widetilde{g}_{2},z)=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{2},z)
.

(3)

J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1}\widetilde{g}_{2},z)=J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g}_{2},z)
.

Proof.

1)
J_{1/2}(g,z)^{2}=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)^{2}t^{2}=\det J(g,z)\alpha_{z_{0}}(g)^{-1}
.

2) Note that
\widetilde{g}_{1}\widetilde{g}_{2}=[g_{1}g_{2},\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})t_{1}t_{2}]
.

\displaystyle J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1}\widetilde{g}_{2},z)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(g_{1}g_{2},z)\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})^{-1}t_{1}^{-1}t_{2}^{-1}

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(g_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(g_{2},z)\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})\beta_{z_{0}}(g_{1}^{h_{-1}},g_{2}^{h_{-1}})^{-1}t_{1}^{-1}t_{2}^{-1}

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{1},g_{2}(z))J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g}_{2},z).

3) It is a consequence of (2).
∎

Corollary 8.14
.

For
\widetilde{g}=(g,t)\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, we have:

•

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)tm_{X^{\ast}}(g)^{-1}=\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}
.

•

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z)=\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}
or
-\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}
.

Let
\widetilde{P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}}^{z_{0}}(\mathbb{R})
,
\widetilde{\operatorname{U}}_{m}^{z_{0}}(\mathbb{C})
denote the inverse images of
P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})
and
\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})
in
\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
respectively. Note that for
p\in P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})
,
m_{X^{\ast}}(p)=1
. Moreover, for
g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, if write
g=p_{g}k_{g}
for
p_{g}\in P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})
and
k_{g}\in\operatorname{U}_{m}(\mathbb{C})
, then
m_{X^{\ast}}(g)=m_{X^{\ast}}(k_{g})
. It is known that

\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(g,p)=1=\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(p,g),\quad\quad g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R}),p\in P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R}).

Hence
\widetilde{P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}}^{z_{0}}(\mathbb{R})\simeq P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\times\mu_{8}
, and

P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\hookrightarrow\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R});p\longmapsto[p,1].

Then there exists the positive Iwasawa decomposition:

\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})=P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\cdot\widetilde{\operatorname{U}}_{m}^{z_{0}}(\mathbb{C}),\quad\quad P^{>0}_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})\cap\widetilde{\operatorname{U}}_{m}^{z_{0}}(\mathbb{C})=1_{2m}.

\widetilde{g}=(g,t_{g})\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R}),\quad\widetilde{g}=p_{g}\widetilde{k_{g}},\quad z_{\widetilde{g}}=z_{p_{g}}.

J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})=t_{g}^{-1}J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})=t_{g}^{-1}J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0}).

J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})(cz_{0}+d)=t_{g}^{-1}J_{3/2}(g,z_{0}).

8.4.
Siegel modular forms:
P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})

Let
p=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
0&(a^{T})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}\in P_{X^{\ast}}(\mathbb{R})
. We write
z_{p}=p(i1_{m})=ba^{T}+iaa^{T}\in\mathbb{H}_{m}
. Recall the notations
A
,
B_{k}
, and
B
from Lemma
7.6
. By (
4.4
)(
4.5
), we have:

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)A(x)

\displaystyle=\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h(a)u(a^{-1}b))A(x)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u(a^{-1}b))A(xa)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}xaa^{-1}ba^{T}x^{T})A(xa)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}e^{\pi ixba^{T}x^{T}}\cdot e^{-\pi xaa^{T}x^{T}}

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}e^{i\pi xz_{p}x^{T}}.

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)B_{k}(x)

\displaystyle=\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(h(a)u(a^{-1}b))B_{k}(x)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u(a^{-1}b))B_{k}(xa)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}xaa^{-1}ba^{T}x^{T})B_{k}(xa)

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}(xa)_{k}e^{i\pi xz_{p}x^{T}};

\displaystyle\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)B(x)

\displaystyle=\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)\begin{pmatrix}B_{1}(x)\\
\vdots\\
B_{m}(x)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=|\det(a)|^{1/2}e^{i\pi xz_{p}x^{T}}(xa)^{T}.

\displaystyle J_{1/2}(p,z_{0})=\epsilon(p;z_{0},z_{0})\cdot|\det J(p,z_{0})|^{1/2}=|\det(a)|^{-1/2}.

\displaystyle J_{3/2}(p,z_{0})=J_{1/2}(p,z_{0})J(p,z_{0})=|\det(a)|^{-1/2}(a^{T})^{-1}.

\displaystyle J_{1/2}(p,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)A(x)=e^{i\pi xz_{p}x^{T}}.

\displaystyle J_{3/2}(p,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p)B(x)=x^{T}e^{i\pi xz_{p}x^{T}}.

8.5.
Siegel modular forms:
\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})

g=p_{g}k_{g}(\textrm{ cf. Section }\ref{Iwa}),\quad\quad z_{g}=z_{p_{g}}.

J_{1/2}(p_{g}k_{g},z_{0})=J_{1/2}(p_{g},k_{g}(z_{0}))J_{1/2}(k_{g},z_{0})\beta_{z_{0}}(p_{g}^{h_{-1}},k_{g}^{h_{-1}})=J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0}).

J_{3/2}(p_{g}k_{g},z_{0})=J_{1/2}(p_{g}k_{g},z_{0})(cz_{0}+d)=(cz_{0}+d)J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0}).

\displaystyle J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A(x)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(p_{g},k_{g})^{-1}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(k_{g})A(x)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(k_{g})A(x)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Lem.}\ref{ABM}}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A(x)

\displaystyle=e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}.

\displaystyle J_{3/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B(x)

\displaystyle=(cz_{0}+d)J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(p_{g},k_{g})^{-1}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(k_{g})B(x)

\displaystyle=(cz_{0}+d)J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})[\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(k_{g})B](x)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Lem.}\ref{ABM}}}{{=}}(cz_{0}+d)J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})[(-\sqrt{y_{g}}cz_{0}+\sqrt{y_{g}}d)^{T}B](x)

\displaystyle=(cz_{0}+d)e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}(-\sqrt{y_{g}}cz_{0}+\sqrt{y_{g}}d)^{T}\sqrt{y_{g}}^{T}x^{T}

\displaystyle=e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}(cz_{0}+d)(-cz_{0}+d)^{T}y_{g}^{T}x^{T}

\displaystyle=e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}x^{T}

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B(x).

8.6.
Siegel modular forms:
\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})

\widetilde{g}=(g,t_{g})\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R}),\quad\widetilde{g}=p_{g}\widetilde{k_{g}},\quad z_{\widetilde{g}}=z_{p_{g}}.

\displaystyle J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(\widetilde{g})A(x)

\displaystyle=t_{g}^{-1}J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})t_{g}\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A(x)

\displaystyle=e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}.

\displaystyle J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(\widetilde{g})B(x)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B(x)

\displaystyle=e^{i\pi xz_{g}x^{T}}x^{T}

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B(x).

8.7.
Siegel modular forms:
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(f)(0)=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}f(l)=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}f(n).

For
g\in\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
, let us define:

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(g)

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A(n)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(g)

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{3/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B(n)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}n^{T}.

Note that
\theta_{1/2}(g)
and
\theta_{3/2}(g)
only depend on
z_{g}=g(z_{0})
. For
z\in\mathbb{H}_{m}
, we define:

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi nzn^{T}},

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}n^{T}e^{i\pi nzn^{T}}.

Let us consider the explicit action of
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
on
\theta_{1/2}(z)
and
\theta_{3/2}(z)
. For
r=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, we have:

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(rg)

\displaystyle=\theta_{1/2}(rp_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(rp_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(rp_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\widetilde{c}_{X^{\ast}}(r,p_{g})=1}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(r,p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(r)\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\ref{chapeq8})}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(r,z_{g})\epsilon_{r}\theta_{1/2}(g)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\det(cz_{g}+d)}m_{X^{\ast}}(r)\epsilon_{r}\theta_{1/2}(g)

\displaystyle=m_{X^{\ast}}(r)\widetilde{\beta}^{-1}(r)\sqrt{\det(cz_{g}+d)}\theta_{1/2}(g)

\displaystyle=\lambda(r)\sqrt{\det(cz_{g}+d)}\theta_{1/2}(g).

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(rg)

\displaystyle=\theta_{3/2}(rp_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(rp_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(rp_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(r,p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(r)\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\ref{chapeq8})}}{{=}}J_{3/2}(r,z_{g})\epsilon_{r}\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(r,z_{g})\epsilon_{r}\theta_{3/2}(g)

\displaystyle=m_{X^{\ast}}(r)\widetilde{\beta}^{-1}(r)\sqrt{\det(cz_{g}+d)}(cz_{g}+d)\theta_{3/2}(g)

\displaystyle=\lambda(r)\sqrt{\det(cz_{g}+d)}(cz_{g}+d)\theta_{3/2}(g).

We conclude:

Theorem 8.15
.

For
r=\begin{pmatrix}a&b\\
c&d\end{pmatrix}\in\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
, we have:

(1)

\theta_{1/2}(rz)=\lambda(r)\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}\theta_{1/2}(z)
.

(2)

\theta_{3/2}(rz)=\lambda(r)\sqrt{\det(cz+d)}(cz+d)\theta_{3/2}(z)
.

8.8.
Siegel modular forms:
\operatorname{Sp}_{2m}(\mathbb{Z})

Let
\widetilde{\Gamma}^{z_{0}}_{m}(1,2)
denote the inverse image of
\Gamma_{m}(1,2)
in
\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
. Let
\widetilde{g}=(g,t_{g})\in\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}^{z_{0}}_{2m}(\mathbb{R})
and
\widetilde{r}=(r,t_{r})\in\widetilde{\Gamma}^{z_{0}}_{m}(1,2)
. Let us define:

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(\widetilde{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}};

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(\widetilde{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(\widetilde{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(g,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(g)B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}n^{T}.

Then:

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{r}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(rg,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(rg)A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(r,z_{g})\epsilon_{r}\theta_{1/2}(g)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{r},z_{g})t_{r}\epsilon_{r}\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{g});

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{r}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(rg,z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(rg)B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(r,z_{g})\epsilon_{r}\theta_{3/2}(g)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(\widetilde{r},z_{g})t_{r}\epsilon_{r}\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{g}).

Recall notations
\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{2}
,
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{2}
,
M_{q}
,
M^{i}_{q}
,
M^{(j,k)}_{q}
from Sections
2.4
–
2.6
. Let us write:

\iota_{i}(\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix})=1_{2m},\quad u_{i}=\iota_{i}(\begin{pmatrix}1&1\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}),\quad u^{-}_{i}=\iota_{i}(\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
-1&1\end{pmatrix}),\quad\omega_{i}=\iota_{i}(\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\
-1&0\end{pmatrix}),\quad n=(n_{1},\cdots,n_{m})\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}.

Note that
u_{i}
,
u_{i}^{-}
are just the previous
u_{ii}
and
u^{-}_{ii}
. Let us write
\epsilon_{1}=(1,0,\cdots,0),\cdots,\epsilon_{m}=(0,\cdots,0,1)
for the natural basis of
\mathbb{R}^{m}
.

8.8.1.
\widetilde{u}_{i}=(u_{i},t_{u_{i}})

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(A)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})=\sum_{l=(l_{1},\cdots,l_{m})\in L\cap X}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}l_{i})A(l)=\sum_{n=(n_{1},\cdots,n_{m})\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}n_{i})A(n).

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}n_{i})\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(n)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{32}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{2}n_{i})\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(n)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}n^{T}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{1/2}(u_{i}p_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(u_{i}p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u_{i}p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(u_{i},p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{i})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 6 }(\ref{ui})}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(u_{i},z_{g})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(u_{i},z_{g})\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i},z_{g})t_{u_{i}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{3/2}(u_{i}p_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(u_{i}p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u_{i}p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(u_{i},p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u_{i})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 6 }(\ref{ui})}}{{=}}J_{3/2}(u_{i},z_{g})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(u_{i},z_{g})\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}n^{T}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i},z_{g})t_{u_{i}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(-1)^{n_{i}}n^{T}e^{i\pi nz_{g}n^{T}}.

8.8.2.
\widetilde{u}_{i}^{-}=(u_{i}^{-},t_{u_{i}^{-}})

Write
u_{i}^{-}=\begin{pmatrix}1_{m}&0\\
c&1_{m}\end{pmatrix}
, for
c=\epsilon_{ii}(-1)
.

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(f)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}f(l+\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})=\sum_{n=(n_{1},\cdots,n_{m})\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}f(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i}).

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}e^{i\pi(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})z_{g}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}e^{i\pi(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})z_{g}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}^{-}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{1/2}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(u^{-}_{i},p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u^{-}_{i})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 7 }(\ref{ui-})}}{{=}}J_{1/2}(u^{-}_{i},z_{g})m_{X^{\ast}}(u^{-}_{i})^{-1}\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{1/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}^{-},z_{g})t_{u_{i}^{-}}m_{X^{\ast}}(u^{-}_{i})^{-1}e^{i\pi(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})z_{g}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}^{-}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{3/2}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(u^{-}_{i}p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(u^{-}_{i},p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u^{-}_{i})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 7 }(\ref{ui-})}}{{=}}J_{3/2}(u^{-}_{i},z_{g})m_{X^{\ast}}(u^{-}_{i})^{-1}\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(\tfrac{1}{2}e_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{3/2}(\widetilde{u}_{i}^{-},z_{g})t_{u_{i}^{-}}m_{X^{\ast}}(u^{-}_{i})^{-1}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}e^{i\pi(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})z_{g}(n+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{i})^{T}}.

8.8.3.
\widetilde{M_{q}^{(j,k)}}

Let
u=M_{q}^{(j,k)}
,
t_{u}=t_{M_{q}^{(j,k)}}
and
\widetilde{u}=(u,t_{u})
.

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}(f)(-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in L\cap X}f(l-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})\psi(\langle l,-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}\rangle)\psi(\tfrac{\langle-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k},-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}\rangle}{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=(n_{1},\cdots,n_{m})\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}f(n-\tfrac{1}{2}(\epsilon_{j}+\epsilon_{k}))\psi(-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{k})\psi(\tfrac{1}{4}).

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(n-\tfrac{1}{2}(\epsilon_{j}+\epsilon_{k}))\psi(-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{k})\psi(\tfrac{1}{4})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4})(-1)^{n_{j}+n_{k}}e^{i\pi(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})z_{g}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(n-\tfrac{1}{2}(\epsilon_{j}+\epsilon_{k}))\psi(-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}n_{k})\psi(\tfrac{1}{4})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}\psi(\tfrac{1}{4})(-1)^{n_{j}+n_{k}}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}e^{i\pi(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})z_{g}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{1/2}(\widetilde{u}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{1/2}(up_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(up_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(up_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{1/2}(u,p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u)\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 9 }(\ref{ui-})}}{{=}}\psi(-\tfrac{1}{8})J_{1/2}(u,z_{g})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{1/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})A\Big)(-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{1/2}(\widetilde{u},z_{g})t_{u}m_{X^{\ast}}(u)^{-1}(-1)^{n_{j}+n_{k}}e^{i\pi(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})z_{g}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}}.

\displaystyle\theta_{3/2}(\widetilde{u}\widetilde{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{3/2}(up_{g})

\displaystyle=\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(up_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(up_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle=J_{3/2}(u,p_{g}(z_{0}))\pi_{L,\psi}(u)\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{Case 9 }(\ref{ui-})}}{{=}}\psi(-\tfrac{1}{8})J_{3/2}(u,z_{g})\theta_{L,X^{\ast}}\Big(J_{3/2}(p_{g},z_{0})\pi_{X^{\ast},\psi}(p_{g})B\Big)(-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k}^{\ast}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}e_{k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{m}}J_{3/2}(\widetilde{u},z_{g})t_{u}m_{X^{\ast}}(u)^{-1}(-1)^{n_{j}+n_{k}}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}e^{i\pi(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})z_{g}(n-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{k})^{T}}.

8.8.4.

Recall the notations
M_{q}
,
M_{q}^{i}
,
M_{q}^{(j,k)}
,
\mathcal{S}^{q}_{0}=\{i_{1},\cdots,i_{r}\}
,
\mathcal{S}^{q}_{1}=\{(j_{1},k_{1}),\cdots,(j_{s},k_{s})\}
from Sections
2.4
–
2.6
. Write
M_{q}=(\prod_{i\in\mathcal{S}^{q}_{0}}M_{q}^{i})\cdot(\prod_{(j,k)\in\mathcal{S}^{q}_{1}}M_{q}^{(j,k)})
, with
M_{q}^{i}=1_{2m}
,
u_{i}
or
u_{i}^{-}
,
M_{q}^{(j,k)}=\iota_{(j,k)}(u_{1111}^{1}u_{1111}^{2})
.
For each
M_{q}
, let us define:

•
